# Conformance corpus: D mangled names.
#
# Read from the shipped libgphobos and libgdruntime by tools/generate_d_corpus.py,
# with the expected column produced by GNU binutils' D demangler,
# `c++filt --format=dlang`.
#
# This corpus holds the names the scheme currently reads *exactly*. It is a floor,
# not a claim of completeness: D landed the same way as the C-family schemes -- see
# ROADMAP.md for where it stands. What this file does is stop that number going
# down.
#
